\frac{2(1-x)}{x}e^x + \frac{x-2}{x}e^x = e^x \left( \frac{x}{x} + \frac{2-2x}{x} + \frac{x-2}{x} \right) = e^x\cdot 0 = 0$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_indisplay15900}%
$\displaystyle y_2(x) =
y_1(x)\int\frac{W(y_1,y_2)}{y_1^2(x)}\;{\rm d}x = y_1(x)\int\frac{e^{\int p(x)\;{\rm d}x}}{y_1^2(x)}\;{\rm d}x.$%
\lthtmlindisplaymathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15902}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        y_2(x) & = & y_1(x)\int\frac{e^{-\int p(x)\;{\rm d}x}}{y_1^2(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int\frac{e^{-\int \frac{2(1-x)}{x} \;{\rm d}x}}{e^{2x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int\frac{e^{-\int (\frac{2}{x} - 2) \;{\rm d}x}}{e^{2x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int\frac{e^{\ln x^{-2} + 2x}}{e^{2x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int\frac{e^{\ln x^{-2}}e^{2x}}{e^{2x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int x^{-2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -\frac{e^x}{x}
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15904}%
$ y(x) = c_1e^x - c_2\frac{e^x}{x}$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15906}%
$ y_p(x) = u_1(x)y_1(x) + u_2(x)y_2(x)$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_indisplay15908}%
$\displaystyle u_1(x)
= \int \frac{-g(x)y_2(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x.$%
\lthtmlindisplaymathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15910}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        W(y_1,y_2)(x) & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} y_1(x) & y_2(x) \\y_1'(x) & y_2'(x) \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} e^x & \frac{e^x}{x} \\e^x & \frac{e^x}{x} - \frac{e^x}{x^2} \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} e^x & \frac{e^x}{x} \\e^x & \frac{(x-1)e^x}{x^2} \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & e^x\frac{(x-1)e^x}{x^2} - \frac{e^x}{x}e^x \\\\
                      & = & e^{2x}\frac{(x-1)}{x^2} - \frac{1}{x}e^{2x} \\\\
                      & = & e^{2x}\left\lbrack \frac{(x-1)}{x^2} - \frac{1}{x}\right\rbrack \\\\
                      & = & -\frac{e^{2x}}{x^2}.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15912}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_1(x) & = & \int \frac{-g(x)y_2(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{-\frac{2}{x}e^x \cdot \frac{e^x}{x}}{ -\frac{e^{2x}}{x^2} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int 2\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & 2x.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15914}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_2(x) & = & \int \frac{g(x)y_1(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{\frac{2}{x}e^x \cdot e^x}{ -\frac{e^{2x}}{x^2} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int -2x\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -x^2.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15916}%
$ y_p(x) = u_1(x)y_1(x) + u_2(x)y_2(x) = 2x \cdot e^x - x^2 \frac{e^x}{x} = xe^x$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15923}%
$ y(x) = c_1e^x + c_2xe^x$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15929}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        W(y_1,y_2)(x) & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} y_1(x) & y_2(x) \\y_1'(x) & y_2'(x) \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} e^x & xe^x \\e^x & e^x+xe^x \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} e^x & xe^x \\e^x & (x+1)e^x \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & e^x(x+1)e^x - e^xxe^x \\\\
                      & = & e^{2x}\left\lbrack (x+1) - x\right\rbrack \\\\
                      & = & e^{2x}
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15931}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_1(x) & = & \int \frac{-g(x)y_2(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{-\frac{e^x}{1-x}xe^x}{ e^{2x} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{x}{x-1} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{x-1+1}{x-1} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \left(\frac{x-1}{x-1} + \frac{1}{x-1}\right) \;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \left(1+\frac{1}{x-1} \right) \;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & x + \ln|x-1|
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15933}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_2(x) & = & \int \frac{g(x)y_1(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{\frac{e^x}{1-x} e^x}{ e^{2x} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{1}{1-x} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -\ln|1-x|.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15935}%
$ y_p(x) = u_1(x)y_1(x) + u_2(x)y_2(x) = (x + \ln|x-1|) \cdot e^x - \ln|1-x| \cdot xe^x$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15937}%
$ y(x) = c_1e^x + c_2xe^x + (x + \ln|x-1|) \cdot e^x - \ln|1-x| \cdot xe^x$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15940}%
$ y_1(x)=x$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15942}%
$ y_1'' +
\frac{1}{x}y_1' - \frac{1}{x^2}y_1 = 0 +
\frac{1}{x} - \frac{1}{x} = 0$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15948}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        y_2(x) & = & y_1(x)\int\frac{e^{-\int p(x)\;{\rm d}x}}{y_1^2(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & x \int\frac{e^{-\int \frac{1}{x} \;{\rm d}x}}{x^2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & x \int\frac{e^{-\ln|x|}}{x^2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & x \int\frac{e^{-\ln x }}{x^2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & x \int\frac{1}{x^3}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & x \frac{-1}{2x^2} \\\\
               & = & -\frac{1}{2x}
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15950}%
$ y(x) = c_1x + c_2\frac{1}{x}$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15956}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        W(y_1,y_2)(x) & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} y_1(x) & y_2(x) \\y_1'(x) & y_2'(x) \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} x & \frac{1}{x} \\1 & -\frac{1}{x^2} \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & x\frac{-1}{x^2} - \frac{1}{x}\cdot 1 \\\\
                      & = & -\frac{2}{x}.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15958}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_1(x) & = & \int \frac{-g(x)y_2(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{-\frac{1}{x^2+x^3}\cdot \frac{1}{x}}{ -\frac{2}{x} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{2}{x^2+x^3}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{2}{x^2(1+x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \left(\frac{-2}{x} + \frac{2}{x^2} + \frac{2}{1+x} \right)\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -2\ln x - \frac{2}{x} + 2 \ln (1+x) \\\\
               & = & -2\ln x - \frac{1}{x} + 2 \ln (1+x)
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15960}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_2(x) & = & \int \frac{g(x)y_1(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{\frac{1}{x^2+x^3} \cdot x}{ -\frac{2}{x} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -\frac{1}{2}\int \frac{x^2}{x^2+x^3} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -\frac{1}{2}\int \frac{1}{1+x} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -\frac{1}{2}\ln (1+x).
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15962}%
$ y_p(x) = u_1(x)y_1(x) + u_2(x)y_2(x) = \left( -2\ln x - \frac{1}{x} + 2 \ln (1+x) \right) \cdot x - \frac{1}{2}\ln (1+x) \cdot \frac{1}{x}$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15964}%

\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15971}%

\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15975}%

- \frac{2x+1}{x}e^x + \frac{x+1}{x}e^x = e^x \left( \frac{x}{x} - \frac{2x+1}{x} + \frac{x+1}{x} \right) = e^x\cdot 0 = 0$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15982}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        y_2(x) & = & y_1(x)\int\frac{e^{-\int p(x)\;{\rm d}x}}{y_1^2(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int\frac{e^{\int \frac{2x+1}{x} \;{\rm d}x}}{e^{2x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int\frac{e^{\int (2 + \frac{1}{x}) \;{\rm d}x}}{e^{2x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int\frac{e^{2x + \ln x}}{e^{2x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \int x\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & e^x \frac{x^2}{2}
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline15984}%
$ y(x) = c_1e^x + c_2x^2e^x$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15990}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        W(y_1,y_2)(x) & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} y_1(x) & y_2(x) \\y_1'(x) & y_2'(x) \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} e^x & x^2e^x \\e^x & 2xe^x + x^2e^x \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} e^x & x^2e^x \\e^x & (x^2+2x)e^x \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & e^x(x^2+2x)e^x - x^2e^xe^x \\\\
                      & = & e^{2x}\left\lbrack x^2+2x - x^2\right\rbrack \\\\
                      & = & 2xe^{2x}.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath15992}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_1(x) & = & \int \frac{-g(x)y_2(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{-1 \cdot x^2e^x}{ 2xe^{2x} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int -\frac{x}{2e^{x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -\frac{1}{2} \int \frac{x}{e^{x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \frac{1}{2} \int \frac{1-(x+1)}{e^{x}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \frac{1}{2} (x+1)e^{-x}
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage

               & = & \int \frac{1 \cdot e^x}{ 2xe^{2x} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{1}{2xe^x} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \frac{1}{2}\int \frac{1}{xe^x} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ

             & = & c_1e^x + k_2x^2e^x + \frac{x+1}{2} - \frac{1}{2} x^2e^x E(x)
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%

        y_2(x) & = & y_1(x)\int\frac{e^{-\int p(x)\;{\rm d}x}}{y_1^2(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & 1 \int\frac{e^{-\int \tan x \;{\rm d}x}}{1^2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = &  \int e^{-\ln|\sec x|}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = &  \int e^{\ln |\cos x| }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = &  \int e^{\ln \cos x }\;{\rm d}x \\\\

                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} 1 & \sin x \\0 & \cos x \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \cos x.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%

{\newpage\clearpage

\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_1(x) & = & \int \frac{-g(x)y_2(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{-2\sin x \cos x \cdot \sin x}{ \cos x }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int -2\sin^2 x\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int -2\frac{1-\cos 2x}{2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \int \left(\cos 2x - 1 \right)\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \frac{\sin 2x}{2} - x \\\\
               & = & \sin x\cos x - x
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ

            W(y_1,y_2)(x) & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} y_1(x) & y_2(x) \\y_1'(x) & y_2'(x) \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                          & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} x & \frac{1}{x} \\1 & -\frac{1}{x^2} \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                          & = & x \cdot -\frac{1}{x^2} - \frac{1}{x} \cdot 1 \\\\
                          & = & -\frac{2}{x}.
            \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ

{\newpage\clearpage

            u_1(x) & = & \int \frac{-g(x)y_2(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
                   & = & \int \frac{-\frac{1}{x^3} \cdot \frac{1}{x}}{ -\frac{2}{x} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
                   & = & \int \frac{1}{2x^3} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
                   & = & -\frac{1}{4x^2}
            \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ

{\newpage\clearpage

\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
            u_2(x) & = & \int \frac{g(x)y_1(x)}{W(y_1,y_2)(x)}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
                   & = & \int \frac{\frac{1}{x^3} \cdot x}{ -\frac{2}{x} }\;{\rm d}x \\\\
                   & = & \int -\frac{1}{2x} \;{\rm d}x \\\\
                   & = & -\frac{1}{2}\ln x.
            \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline16161}%

= -\frac{1}{4x} - \frac{1}{2x}\ln x$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

= \frac{(1+3t) \pm (3t-1)}{2t}$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_indisplay16187}%
$\displaystyle y_2(t) =
y_1(t)\int\frac{W(y_1,y_2)}{y_1^2(t)}\;{\rm d}t = y_1(t)\int\frac{e^{\int p(t)\;{\rm d}t}}{y_1^2(t)}\;{\rm d}t.$%
\lthtmlindisplaymathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_indisplay16189}%
$\displaystyle y''  - \frac{1+3t}{t}y' +
\frac{3}{t}y = te^{3t}.$%
\lthtmlindisplaymathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16191}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        y_2(t) & = & y_1(t)\int\frac{e^{-\int p(t)\;{\rm d}t}}{y_1^2(t)}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & e^{3t} \int\frac{e^{-\int - \frac{1+3t}{t} \;{\rm d}t}}{e^{6t}}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & e^{3t} \int\frac{e^{\ln t + 3t}}{e^{6t}}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & e^{3t} \int\frac{te^{3t}}{e^{6t}}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & e^{3t} \int te^{-3t} \;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & e^{3t} \left\lbrack t\cdot \frac{-1}{3}e^{-3t} - \int \frac{-1}{3}e^{-3t}\;{\rm d}t \right\rbrack  \\\\
               & = & e^{3t} \left\lbrack \frac{-t}{3}e^{-3t} - \frac{1}{9}e^{-3t} \right\rbrack  \\\\
               & = & \frac{-t}{3} - \frac{1}{9} \\\\
               & = & -\frac{3t+1}{9}
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline16193}%
$ y(x) = c_1e^{3t} + c_2(3t+1)$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline16195}%
$ y_p(t) = u_1(t)y_1(t) + u_2(t)y_2(t)$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_indisplay16197}%
$\displaystyle u_1(t)
= \int \frac{-g(t)y_2(t)}{W(y_1,y_2)(t)}\;{\rm d}t.$%
\lthtmlindisplaymathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16199}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        W(y_1,y_2)(t) & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} y_1(t) & y_2(t) \\y_1'(t) & y_2'(t) \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} e^{3t} & 3t+1 \\3e^{3t} & 3 \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & 3e^{3t} - 3(3t+1)e^{3t} \\\\
                      & = & -9te^{3t}.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16201}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_1(t) & = & \int \frac{-g(t)y_2(t)}{W(y_1,y_2)(t)}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{-te^{3t} \cdot (3t+1)}{ -9te^{3t} }\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{3t+1}{9}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & \frac{t^2}{6} + \frac{t}{9} \\\\
               & = & \frac{3t^2 + 2t}{18}.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16203}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_2(t) & = & \int \frac{g(t)y_1(t)}{W(y_1,y_2)(t)}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{te^{3t} \cdot e^{3t}}{ -9te^{3t} }\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & \int -\frac{1}{9}e^{3t}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & -\frac{1}{27}e^{3t}.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline16205}%
$ y_p(t) = u_1(t)y_1(t) + u_2(t)y_2(t) = \frac{3t^2 + 2t}{18} \cdot e^{3t} - \frac{1}{27}e^{3t} (3t+1)
= \left( \frac{t^2}{6} + \frac{3}{27}t - \frac{3}{27}t - \frac{1}{27}\right)e^{3t} = \left( \frac{t^2}{6} - \frac{1}{27}\right)e^{3t}$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

        y_3    & = & vy_1 \\
        y_3'   & = & v'y_1 + vy_1' \\
        y_3''  & = & (v''y_1 + v'y_1') + (v'y_1'+vy_1'') = v''y_1 + 2v'y_1' + vy_1'' \\
        y_3''' & = & (v'''y_1 + v''y_1') + 2(v''y_1' + v'y_1'') + (v'y_1''+vy_1''') \\
               & = & v'''y_1 + 3v''y_1' + 3v'y_1'' + vy_1'''
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ

{\newpage\clearpage

        0 & = & y_3''' + ay_3'' + by_3' + cy_3 \\
          & = & (v'''y_1 + 3v''y_1' + 3v'y_1'' + vy_1''') + a(v''y_1 + 2v'y_1' + vy_1'') \\
          &   & \quad\quad\quad+ b(v'y_1 + vy_1') + c(vy_1) \\
          & = & v(y_1''' + ay_1'' + by_1' + cy_1) + v'(3y_1'' + 2ay_1' + by_1) \\
          &   & \quad\quad\quad  + v''(3y_1' + ay_1) + v'''(y_1) \\
          & = & v'(3y_1'' + 2ay_1' + by_1) + v''(3y_1' + ay_1) + v'''(y_1)
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ

          & = & \frac{{\rm d^{2}}}{{\rm d}{x}^{2}}(v') + \frac{3x' + 0 \cdot x}{x}\frac{{\rm d^{}}}{{\rm d}{x}^{}}(v') + \frac{3x'' + 2\cdot 0 \cdot x' - \frac{3}{x^2} x}{x}v' \\\\

{\newpage\clearpage

               & = & 2x \int\frac{e^{-\int \frac{3}{x} \;{\rm d}x}}{4x^2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & 2x \int\frac{e^{-3\ln x}}{4x^2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & 2x \int\frac{x^{-3}}{4x^2}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & \frac{x}{2} \int x^{-5}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & -\frac{x}{8} x^{-4} \\\\

        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%

               & = & x^{\frac{1-a}{2}} \int\frac{x^{-a}}{x^{1-a}}\;{\rm d}x \\\\
               & = & x^{\frac{1-a}{2}} \int x^{-1} \;{\rm d}x \\\\

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16342}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        x^{\lambda_1}   & = & x^{\alpha + i\beta} \\
                        & = & x^{\alpha}x^{i\beta} \\
                        & = & e^{\ln |x^{\alpha}|}e^{\ln |x^{i\beta}|} \\
                        & = & e^{\alpha \ln |x|}e^{{i\beta}\ln |x|} \\
                        & = & e^{\alpha \ln |x|}\left\lbrack \cos \beta\ln |x| + i\sin \beta\ln |x| \right\rbrack
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16344}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        x^{\lambda_2}   & = & x^{\alpha - i\beta} \\
                        & = & x^{\alpha}x^{-i\beta} \\
                        & = & e^{\ln |x^{\alpha}|}e^{\ln |x^{-i\beta}|} \\
                        & = & e^{\alpha \ln |x|}e^{{-i\beta}\ln |x|} \\
                        & = & e^{\alpha \ln |x|}\left\lbrack \cos -\beta\ln |x| + i\sin -\beta\ln |x| \right\rbrack \\
                        & = & e^{\alpha \ln |x|}\left\lbrack \cos \beta\ln |x| - i\sin \beta\ln |x| \right\rbrack \\
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

        6 \ln e^t & = & (e^t)^2y''(e^t) -2e^ty'(e^t) + 2y(e^t) \\

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_indisplay16427}%
$\displaystyle y'' -
\frac{2}{t}y' + \frac{2}{t^2}y = 6\frac{\ln t}{t^2}.$%
\lthtmlindisplaymathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16429}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        W(y_1,y_2)(t) & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} y_1(t) & y_2(t) \\y_1'(t) & y_2'(t) \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & \left\vert \begin{array}{cc} t & t^2 \\1 & 2t \end{array} \right\vert \\\\
                      & = & 2t^2 - t^2 \\\\
                      & = & t^2.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16431}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_1(t) & = & \int \frac{-g(t)y_2(t)}{W(y_1,y_2)(t)}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{-6\frac{\ln t}{t^2} \cdot t^2}{ t^2 }\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & -6 \int \frac{\ln t}{ t^2 }\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & -6 \left\lbrack -\frac{\ln t}{ t } + \int \frac{1}{t^2} \;{\rm d}t  \right\rbrack \\\\
               & = & -6 \left\lbrack -\frac{\ln t}{ t } - \frac{1}{t} \right\rbrack \\\\
               & = & \frac{6}{t} (1+ \ln t).
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16433}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        u_2(t) & = & \int \frac{g(t)y_1(t)}{W(y_1,y_2)(t)}\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & \int \frac{6\frac{\ln t}{t^2} \cdot t}{ t^2 }\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & 6 \int \frac{\ln t}{ t^3 }\;{\rm d}t \\\\
               & = & 6 \left\lbrack -\frac{\ln t}{ 2t^2 } + \int \frac{1}{2t^3} \;{\rm d}t  \right\rbrack \\\\
               & = & 6 \left\lbrack -\frac{\ln t}{ 2t^2 } - \frac{1}{4t^2} \right\rbrack \\\\
               & = & -\frac{3}{2t^2} (1+ 2\ln t).
\par
\end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmlinlinemathA{tex2html_wrap_inline16435}%
$ y_p(t) = u_1(t)y_1(t) + u_2(t)y_2(t) = \frac{6}{t} (1+ \ln t) \cdot t - \frac{3}{2t^2} (1+ 2\ln
t) \cdot t^2  = 6(1+\ln t) -\frac{3}{2} (1+2\ln t) = \frac{9}{2} + 3\ln t$%
\lthtmlinlinemathZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16448}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        y_{p_1}'' + 2y_{p_1}' + y_{p_1} & = & \left( -\frac{6}{25}\cos 2x + \frac{8}{25}\sin 2x \right)'' \\
                                        &   & \quad\quad + 2\left( -\frac{6}{25}\cos 2x + \frac{8}{25}\sin 2x \right)' \\
                                        &   & \quad\quad\quad\quad + \left( -\frac{6}{25}\cos 2x + \frac{8}{25}\sin 2x \right) \\\\
                                        & = & \left( \frac{24}{25}\cos 2x - \frac{32}{25}\sin 2x \right) \\
                                        &   & \quad\quad + 2\left( \frac{12}{25}\sin 2x + \frac{16}{25}\cos 2x \right) \\
                                        &   & \quad\quad\quad\quad + \left( -\frac{6}{25}\cos 2x + \frac{8}{25}\sin 2x \right) \\\\
                                        & = & 2\cos 2x.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16468}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        3xe^{-x} & = & y_{p_3}'' + 2y_{p_3}' + y_{p_3} \\\\
                 & = & \left\lbrack (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack'' \\
                 &   & \quad\quad + 2\left\lbrack (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack' \\
                 &   & \quad\quad\quad\quad + \left\lbrack (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack \\\\
                 & = & \left\lbrack (2A_2x + 3A_3x^2)e^{-x} - (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack' \\
                 &   & \quad\quad + 2\left\lbrack (2A_2x + 3A_3x^2)e^{-x} - (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack \\
                 &   & \quad\quad\quad\quad + \left\lbrack (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack \\\\
                 & = & \left\lbrack (2A_2 + 6A_3x)e^{-x} - (2A_2x + 3A_3x^2)e^{-x} \right\rbrack \\
                 &   & \quad\quad \left\lbrack -(2A_2x + 3A_3x^2)e^{-x} + (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack \\
                 &   & \quad\quad\quad\quad + 2\left\lbrack (2A_2x + 3A_3x^2)e^{-x} - (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack \\
                 &   & \quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad + \left\lbrack (A_2x^2 + A_3x^3)e^{-x} \right\rbrack \\\\
                 & = & \left( 0 \right) x^3e^{-x} + \left( 0 \right) x^2e^{-x} + \left( 6A_3 \right) xe^{-x} + \left( 2A_2 \right) e^{-x}.
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}

{\newpage\clearpage

\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rclcl}
        \frac{e^{it} + e^{-it}}{2} & = & \frac{(\cos t + i\sin t) + (\cos -t + i\sin-t)}{2}& = & \cos t \\\\
        \frac{e^{it} - e^{-it}}{2i} & = & \frac{(\cos t + i\sin t) - (\cos -t + i\sin-t)}{2i}& = & \sin t \\\\
        t\frac{e^{it} + e^{-it}}{2} & = & t\frac{(\cos t + i\sin t) + (\cos -t + i\sin-t)}{2}& = & t\cos t \\\\
        t\frac{e^{it} - e^{-it}}{2i} & = & t\frac{(\cos t + i\sin t) - (\cos -t + i\sin-t)}{2i}& = & t\sin t
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%

{\newpage\clearpage

{\newpage\clearpage
\lthtmldisplayA{displaymath16513}%
\begin{displaymath}\begin{array}[t]{rcl}
        W(y_1,y_2,y_3,y_4)(t) & = & W(\sin t, t\sin t, \cos t, t\cos t) \\\\
                              & = & Ce^{-\int p(t)\;{\rm d}t} \\\\
                              & = & Ce^{-\int 0\;{\rm d}t} \\\\
                              & = & Ce^{-k}
        \end{array}\end{displaymath}%
\lthtmldisplayZ
\lthtmlcheckvsize\clearpage}
